# Youch! build pack

Everything an AI coding agent needs to rebuild the Youch! app UI in the Circle & Line style for Web (HTML and CSS): the brief, the design tokens, the real code that draws the UI kit and screens and the logo, with a prompt at the top that says how to use it. Generated by Looks Good.

## Part 0: How to use this file (prompt for the agent)

This section is addressed to you, the AI coding agent. Read it first and follow it exactly. It tells you what this file is, which part wins when two disagree, what to do in what order, and what to report when you finish.

**What this file is.** The build pack for Youch!: App UI, Circle & Line style, for Web (HTML and CSS). It holds the brand brief (Part 1), the design tokens (Part 2), the real CSS and reference markup that draw every UI kit component (Part 3) and every screen layout (Part 4), the logo (Part 6) and a checklist you must pass (Part 7). Everything you need is here; do not redraw anything from memory and do not invent values this file does not give.

**Authority order.** When two things in this file disagree, the earlier one wins:

1. Part 1 brief rules: what is allowed, where, and why.
2. Part 2 tokens: the only colours, type faces and spacing values you may use.
3. Part 3 component CSS values (`kit.css`): every colour, size, radius, border, shadow and type setting of every component.
4. Part 3 and Part 4 reference markup: illustrative. It shows structure and intent; class names are Looks Good's own, and the demo copy ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz") is placeholder text.

**Steps.**

1. Save this file in the project root as `BUILD-PACK.md`, and read Part 1 completely before writing code.
2. Create the token file from Part 2 (`tokens.css`: CSS custom properties, light and dark) in the project's stack (plain CSS, Tailwind theme, CSS modules). Keep the token names; every colour in the project comes from them.
3. Build each UI kit component from Part 3 by porting the `kit.css` values exactly into the project's styling system. You may rename the Looks Good class names (`ab-*`, `sb-k-*`, `sb-*`) to the project's own conventions, but keep every value: colours, sizes, radii, borders, shadows, type, spacing and every state (rest, pressed, focus, disabled, open and so on). The selectors are scoped to the kit root described in Part 3; keep the same scoping or fold it into your own. The custom properties `kit.css` reads are resolved in Part 2.
4. Assemble screens from Part 4: each layout names its blocks in order, its rules, and gives the reference markup of the phone screen. Spacing, the 44pt tap target and the type scale come from Part 1 and do not change per screen.
5. Patterns are out of scope for this build: it is an App, and app screens carry no patterns or art.
6. Place the logo from Part 6 at the sizes and positions Part 1 gives.
7. Finish with the Part 7 checklist: go through every item, fix what fails, and do not call the work done until all of it passes.

**Hard rules** (they repeat Part 1 on purpose).

- No colour outside the tokens. No gradient, opacity or tint that is not derived from them as Part 1 says.
- No art and no patterns in app UI: app screens are built from the UI kit only.
- The demo copy in the reference markup ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz" and the like) is placeholder: replace it with the product's real text, but keep the structure and length discipline.
- Keep the 44pt minimum tap target and the phone type scale (sizes, line heights, weights) exactly as Part 1 gives them.
- Match both light and dark exactly as the tokens define them; the dark palette is designed, not inverted.

**Report back** when you finish: (1) the files you created or changed; (2) which Part 7 items pass, and for any that do not, why; (3) every place you could not match a Part 3 value or a Part 1 rule, and what you used instead; (4) any placeholder copy you left in.

## Part 1: Brand brief

The full AI brief for this brand, unchanged apart from its headings being one step deeper. It holds the rules: colours and where each is used, type, shape, states, motion, accessibility and what is allowed where. Where anything in this file conflicts with it, the brief wins.

### Youch! — build brief
Build the real product from this brief, not a mock-up. Sections are marked Locked, Guided or Free. Locked values are final: use only these colors, fonts, shapes and motion, and where one is missing use the nearest listed token. Guided sections give an intent and a range; stay inside them. Free means design it for this product: layout, screen structure and how many sections a screen has. Screens are composed from the UI kit below, one component for each need; do not invent new component styles, and do not copy the brand board's layout. Target: a phone app in HTML and CSS, laid out in iOS points (1pt = 1 CSS px).
Youch!: Simple things, done well. Aesthetic: Circle & Line (Round · Inked · Calm; Bauhaus geometry (Albers), Dieter Rams and Braun instrument panels, Swiss modernism). Every control is a flat round pill or a perfect circle drawn with one ink line, and choosing something fills it solid periwinkle. One line, one circle, one choice.
Product: Youch!. Hold a key, talk, and your words land where you're typing. It runs on this Mac, tidies what you said and never rewrites it.

#### App UI rules (Guided)
Build every screen from the UI kit only. The brand shows up through colour, type, corners, edges, shadow and how each component is drawn. Never put the board's hero illustration, deck art, patterns, textures, decorative bands or header art into app screens. The logo appears at icon size in the top bar or on the welcome screen, nowhere else. Images appear only when they are content (a user photo, a product photo). Never introduce a colour that is not in the palette: destructive and status colours come from it too.
The brand board, deck and landing page show art for the brand's identity and marketing; none of it belongs in the app.
Screens are composed from the UI kit and the phone layout below: pick the component for the need, set text in the type scale, and leave the rest to the tokens.
Focus rings appear on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web, the system focus on SwiftUI), never on tap or click; disabled controls take no focus and no press response.

##### How Circle & Line shows up in the app
- Corners: buttons pill, fields pill, cards 20pt, badges pill.
- Surfaces: card --canvas with a 1pt solid --line edge; list rows unfilled; bottom sheet --canvas with a 1pt solid --line edge.
- Type: welcome title Outfit (display) 600, tracking -0.04em; section labels Inter (body) 600, uppercase, tracking +0.08em; button labels Inter (body) 600.
- Primary and selection: the primary button is --primary with a --on-primary label; the selected segment is --primary; the selected tab shows a 16x3pt --primary pill, near the bottom of the box, centred.
- Signature details: toggle: a 21pt --canvas dot, vertically centred in the box, at the left; pie menu: a 152pt --ink at 3% dot with a 1pt solid --line edge, vertically centred in the box, centred; skeleton screen: a smooth fade of --canvas at 55% at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line).

Where this aesthetic's colour rules and a component description disagree, the colour rules win.
Palette colours no app component draws (brand board and app icon only; they stay in `:root`): `--secondary` #dde1f2, `--accent-ink` #101116 / #f6cf6b, `--sec-tint` #f5f6f6 / #313239, `--info-icon` #717dac / #8290c7, `--kit-stepper` #3b3a38.
- Primary budget: `--primary` appears only where the UI kit draws it (Primary button, Text field, Search field, Checkbox, Radio, Toggle, Segmented control, List rows, Tab bar, Tabs, Split button, Floating button, Combo box, Listbox, Date picker, Calendar picker, Navigation bar, Navigation menu, Drawer menu, Anchor links, Contextual menu, Expandable menu, Ribbon, Pie menu), never as extra fills, text or rules. Selection, toggles, tabs and focus are drawn as their kit rows say, not in extra primary.
- Highlight word: use the brand's tagline unchanged as the welcome title (never reword it) and highlight its last word together with any full stop, wrapped in one span with `white-space: nowrap`; draw it as the Built Welcome screen does: an underline in --accent, 0.47em thick, 0em below the baseline.
- Text colours are the ones each UI kit row states; no other colours, tints or gradients on text.

#### UI kit (Locked)
Measured from the UI kit tab as Circle & Line draws it. Every control and container in the product is one of these components, drawn exactly as stated. Colours are tokens from Colors (a plain hex or rgba() is a fixed value the kit draws); "`--x` at N%" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, transparent)` and "at N% over `--canvas`" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, var(--canvas))`; sizes are points at the 390 x 844 reference (1pt = 1 CSS px); padding is CSS shorthand; exact shadow values are the `--shadow-*` tokens; (display) and (body) name the brand faces. "Other states" lists only what changes from the first state.

| Tab bar | 3 to 5 top-level destinations at the bottom of a phone. | none | per tab, as the tab bar row lists | no edge; square corners | 83pt high on a screen, including the 34pt home indicator area | selected tab: --ink, Inter (body) 10pt 600, 47pt high; selected tab: a 16x3pt --primary pill, near the bottom of the box, centred; unselected tab: --kit-primary-2, Inter (body) 10pt 500, 47pt high | one state |
| Tabs | In-page sections under a title. | none | --on-primary, Inter (body) 15pt 600 | bottom 1pt solid --line; square corners | 45pt high | selected: fill --primary, edge 1pt solid --ink; selected: a 69x2pt --primary bar, near the bottom of the box, centred; unselected: fill --canvas, --ink, Inter (body) 15pt 400, edge 1pt solid --ink, 14pt corners, 44pt high | one state |
| Navigation menu | A vertical list of destinations with the current one marked. | none | --ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | no edge; square corners | fits its content | row: fill --primary at 12%, edge 1pt solid --line, 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt; selected row: fill --primary at 12%, --ink, Inter (body) 17pt 400, edge 1pt solid --line, 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt; title: --ink, Inter (body) 17pt 500; icon tile: fill --pri-tint, 10pt corners, 32pt high | one state |
| Drawer menu | Navigation hidden behind a menu button, sliding in from the side. | --canvas | --ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | 1pt solid --line; corners 0 20pt 20pt 0 (clockwise from top-left) | padding 12pt 8pt | scrim: fill --ink at 6% over --canvas, 16pt corners; scrim: a --black at 38% sheet covering the whole box; selected row: fill --primary at 12%, --ink, Inter (body) 17pt 400, edge 1pt solid --line, 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt; row: edge 1pt solid --line, 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt | one state |
| Scrollbar | Shows where you are in content that scrolls, and drags it. | none | --ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | no edge; square corners | fits its content | thumb: fill --ink, 71pt high | one state |

| Contextual menu | Actions for one item, on long press or right click. | --canvas | --ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | 1pt solid --line; 20pt corners | fits its content | chosen option: --ink, Inter (body) 17pt 600; target row: fill --primary at 12%, edge bottom 1pt solid --line, 59pt high, padding 8pt 16pt | one state |

| Avatar | A person or account: photo or initials. | --canvas | --ink, Outfit (display) 13pt 400, uppercase | no edge; circle | 32x32pt | none | one state |

#### Product screens (Guided)
These are Youch!'s own screens, from its product sheet. What gets checked is the content ranking (1 is the most important), where the style's signature lands, and the fold budget; the layout itself is designed for the product from the kit's components, with no pixel positions given. Comps of these screens are viewable in Looks Good only.
Platform: Mac. Each screen is a Mac app window, 1280x800 (comps are 2560x1600 at 2x); content taller than the window is drawn in full with a fold line at 800.

##### History
- Job: Find a past dictation, compare what was pasted with what you said, and fix a word once so Youch! learns it.
- Primary action: Replay audio
- Chrome: Sidebar source list (History, Dictionary, Insights, Recordings, Settings), History selected
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "History", highlight word "History".
  2. List group "Today" with 3 rows: "> Claude" (2:14 PM, 312 words · 2 min); "Mail" (11:40 AM, 141 words · 55 sec); "Messages" (9:30 AM, 24 words · 9 sec).
  3. The one primary button "Replay audio", with "2:08".
  4. Card "Claude · 2:14 PM", with "312 words · 2 min · Light", body "So the plan for Pramand is to ship the dictionary first. Then history, then the cleanup dial.".
  5. Card "What you said", body "~~um~~ so the plan for prominent is to ~~uh~~ ship the dictionary first, oh actually I meant Pramand. Then history, then the cleanup dial.".
  6. callout "Learned: prominent → Pramand. Youch! spells it that way from now on.".
- Signature placement: the selected row.
- Fold budget: The list and the opened dictation fit in the window; the What you said card starts above the fold.
- States:
  - Empty: No dictations yet. One line: hold Right Command and talk; the first one shows up here.
  - Loading: none; everything is on this Mac.
  - Error: Audio missing for an old dictation: the text stays, Replay is disabled with a one-line reason.
  - First run: same as Empty.

##### Settings
- Job: Choose how much Youch! tidies and rewords, and the few switches that change how it behaves.
- Primary action: none on this screen
- Chrome: Sidebar source list (History, Dictionary, Insights, Recordings, Settings), Settings selected
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Settings", highlight word "Settings".
  2. Segmented control "Cleanup" with options "Light", "Medium", "Heavy", "Light" selected.
  3. Muted caption "Light removes fillers and fixes punctuation. Nothing else changes.".
  4. Segmented control "Rewording" with options "None", "Low", "Medium", "High", "None" selected.
  5. Toggle row "Show the Youch! button", on, with "A small button at the bottom of the screen. Click it to start or stop dictating.".
  6. Toggle row "Fix spoken corrections at Light too", off, with "Medium and Heavy always do this. “Eight, no wait, five” becomes “five”.".
  7. Toggle row "Launch at login", on.
  8. List group "Styles by app" with 3 rows: "Claude" (Light); "Mail" (Medium); "Messages" (Light).
  9. Secondary button "Export everything", with "Into a folder you can read without Youch!".
- Signature placement: the chosen level on the Cleanup dial.
- Fold budget: Both dials and the three switches fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: none.
  - Loading: none.
  - Error: A model failed to load: ink icon and one line under Models, never red.
  - First run: none.

##### Insights
- Job: Show how you think out loud: how much you've said, when you lose the thread, and your filler habits.
- Primary action: none on this screen
- Chrome: Sidebar source list (History, Dictionary, Insights, Recordings, Settings), Insights selected
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Insights", highlight word "Insights".
  2. Hero numeral "48,210", labelled "Words so far", with "+3,904 this week".
  3. Segmented control "Time range" with options "7 days", "30 days", "All", "7 days" selected.
  4. Sparkline "When you lose the thread" in the style's chart colour, 12 points from 0 to 0.
  5. Stat row "Filler habits" with um: 41; like: 27; you know: 12.
  6. List group "By app" with 3 rows: "Claude" (31,402 words); "Mail" (9,118 words); "Messages" (4,530 words).
  7. Muted caption "Longer dictations are where threads usually go.".
- Signature placement: the Words so far numeral.
- Fold budget: The numeral, the range and the thread chart fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: Fewer than 3 dictations: one line saying insights start after a few dictations.
  - Loading: none; worked out on this Mac.
  - Error: none.
  - First run: same as Empty.

#### Phone layout and type (Guided)
| Role | Face | Size pt | Line | Weight |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Display | Outfit (display), tracking -0.04em | 34 | 41 | 600 |
| Eyebrow | Inter (body), uppercase, tracking +0.08em | 13 | 18 | 400 |
| Label | Inter (body), uppercase, tracking +0.08em | 13 | 18 | 600 |
| Large title | Outfit (display), tracking -0.02em | 34 | 41 | 600 |
| Title | Outfit (display), tracking -0.02em | 28 | 34 | 600 |
| Title 2 | Outfit (display), tracking -0.02em | 22 | 28 | 600 |
| Headline | Inter (body) | 17 | 22 | 500 |
| Body | Inter (body) | 17 | 24 | 400 |
| Callout | Inter (body) | 16 | 22 | 400 |
| Subhead | Inter (body) | 15 | 20 | 400 |
| Footnote | Inter (body) | 13 | 18 | 400 |
| Caption | Inter (body) | 12 | 16 | 600 |
| Tab label | Inter (body) | 10 | 12 | 600 selected, 500 unselected |
- Phone chrome: the status bar takes 54pt at the top and the home indicator 34pt at the bottom; nothing tappable sits in either. The top bar is 44pt high, under the status bar. At rest it shows only the back button and icon actions, with the Large title below it in the content; the title appears in the top bar (Headline size) only once the Large title has scrolled under it.
- Title colours: welcome title --ink, screen titles --ink, eyebrows --ab-muted.
- Layout: 20pt side margins on every screen. Spacing comes from 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48pt only; 28pt between sections. Sizes: buttons 52pt, fields 48pt, list rows 52pt with one line and 59pt with a detail line, card insides as the Home card in the sample screens, tab bar 83pt, nothing tappable under 44pt.
- Support Dynamic Type; never let a word split or a label truncate; one Large title per screen.

#### Light and dark (Locked)
The default look is light, as the sample screens draw it: `color-scheme: light` and the light values on `:root`. Dark is used only when the system asks for it (`[data-theme="dark"]`, set from `prefers-color-scheme: dark` by the script under Tokens). The dark values are designed, not inverted. Text on the canvas uses `--ink` / `--muted`, with each mode's value from the Colors table.

#### States (Locked)
| State | Colour | Visual |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pressed and disabled | as each UI kit row states | The "Other states" column of the UI kit is the one definition; nothing else changes on press. Disabled controls take no press response. |
| Focus | `--primary` where it holds 3:1 on the canvas, else `--ink` | Keyboard focus on any control: a 2pt ring with a 2pt gap, following the control's corners; exactly as the "focus" state of the Primary and Secondary button rows in the UI kit draws it. Shown on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web), never on tap or click; never removed. Text fields show their own focus state from the UI kit instead. |
| Empty | `--ink` headline, `--muted` copy | The Empty state row of the UI kit. |
| Loading | `--line` track, `--primary` fill | Indeterminate bar, or skeleton blocks in `--line` easing between 100% and 60% opacity every 1.2 s. Show it after 300 ms of waiting, with a text label for screen readers. |
| Error | `--error` | The Text field row's error state, with the message under the field. |

#### Motion (Guided)
- Durations: fast 120 ms (press, toggle), base 160 ms (color, selection, shadow), slow 240 ms (sheets, page changes). Easing: ease-out `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1)`.
- Reduce Motion: Replace slides and fades with an instant state swap; colour changes stay. State changes stay; only the movement goes. Nothing else animates idly or loops except loading indicators.

#### Accessibility (Locked)
- Contrast kept (light | dark): ink on canvas 18.0 | 18.0 (AAA); muted on canvas 4.9 | 6.9 (AA); on-primary on primary 5.9 | 5.9 (AA); on-accent on accent 12.3 | 12.3 (AAA); error on canvas 18.0 | 18.0 (AAA). Keep every text pair at 4.5:1 or better (3:1 for the accent-ink highlight word); do not swap tokens in a way that breaks them.
- Every tappable target is at least 44x44 CSS px even when the visible shape is smaller, with 8pt between neighbours. Text scales: sizes in rem, no fixed-height text boxes; layouts reflow at the largest accessibility size (stack, wrap, never truncate a primary label).
- Reduce Transparency (`prefers-reduced-transparency`): translucent fills in the kit (overlay, skeleton screen) become opaque. Increase Contrast (`prefers-contrast: more`): Hairlines become 2pt `--ink`, `--muted` text becomes `--ink`, tints get a 1pt `--ink` edge.
- Reduce Motion (`prefers-reduced-motion`): see Motion. Never color alone: selection, status and errors also use an icon, text or shape; icon-only buttons carry an accessibility label.

#### Icons (Locked)
1.5px strokes, round caps, 20px, drawn in the ink line weight; the selected icon sits in a solid primary circle. Web: inline SVG on a 24px grid, `currentColor`.
- Sizes as the sample screens draw them: 24pt tab bar icons over their 10pt labels; 20pt icons in icon buttons, centred in a 44x44pt hit area; 18pt icons inside the 32pt feature-row chips; 16pt chevrons in the trailing slot of rows, never a text glyph (no ›, > or →); 24pt anywhere else.
- Glyphs the kit uses (draw each once, same stroke): back, more, notifications (bell), search, chevron right, chevron down, check, plus, info, success, warning, error, inbox (empty state), home, list, heart, user.

#### Fonts to bundle (Locked)
- Outfit: 400 (UI kit), 400 italic (UI kit), 600 (Display, Large title, Title, Title 2, UI kit), 600 italic (UI kit).
- Inter: 400 (Eyebrow, Body, Callout, Subhead, Footnote, UI kit), 500 (Headline, UI kit), 600 (Label, Caption, Tab label, UI kit).
- Geist Mono: 600 (UI kit).
- System faces, no file to bundle: -apple-system.
- Bundle exactly these weights and no others; the type scale uses no more. Web: https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Geist+Mono:wght@600&family=Inter:opsz,wght@14..32,400;14..32,500;14..32,600&family=Outfit:wght@400;600&display=swap

#### Signature and product imagery (Guided)
- Embedded product screenshots, app icons and product imagery keep their own colours. They are exempt from the canvas and colour coverage budget and from the accent and primary limits; the studio frame around them (surfaces, captions, borders) follows the brand rules.
- Coverage and whitespace targets apply to the brand's own surfaces, not to product pages that showcase a product's own visual system.

#### Do and don't (Guided)
- Do: Draw every control as a fully round pill or a circle with a 1.5px ink line.
- Do: Fill solid periwinkle only for the one main action, the selected nav item, the selected segment, a switch that is on and the selection wash.
- Do: Keep cards, lists and callouts flat: radius 20, 1px hairline, no shadow.
- Do: Use a light ink line in dark mode so every outline, track and focus ring reads.
- Do: Show errors and warnings as ink with an icon and a plain sentence.
- Do: Keep body Inter 14-15px and list rows compact; text 4.5:1, controls 3:1.
- Don't: Use periwinkle on card fills or body text.
- Don't: Use butter as text colour, a button fill or an outline.
- Don't: Make the switch off track faint: it must read at 3:1.

#### Tokens (CSS) (Locked)
```css
:root {
  color-scheme: light;
  --canvas: #fafaf7; --ink: #101116; --muted: #6e6e70;
  --line: #d7d7d5; --primary: #8290c7; --on-primary: #141414;
  --accent: #f6cf6b; --on-accent: #141414; --pri-tint: #ecedf1;
  --error: #101116; --on-error: #fafaf7; --kit-primary: #6b76a3;
  --white: #ffffff; --kit-primary-2: #46464a; --kit-field: #101116;
  --kit-field-2: #c62f2f; --ab-muted: #5b5b5e; --success-icon: #81838f;
  --warning-icon: #9a8349; --kit-badge: #101116; --primary-text: #66709b;
  --black: #000000; --kit-stepper-text: #3b3a38; --kit-feature: #606a92;
  --secondary: #dde1f2; --on-secondary: #141414; --accent-ink: #101116;
  --sec-tint: #f5f6f6; --info-icon: #717dac; --kit-stepper: #3b3a38;
  --font-heading: "Outfit", -apple-system, sans-serif; --font-body: "Inter", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --font-geist-mono: "Geist Mono", ui-monospace, monospace;
  --text-display: 34px; --text-eyebrow: 13px; --text-label: 13px; --text-large-title: 34px; --text-title: 28px; --text-title-2: 22px; --text-headline: 17px; --text-body: 17px; --text-callout: 16px; --text-subhead: 15px; --text-footnote: 13px; --text-caption: 12px; --text-tab-label: 10px;
  --space-4: 4px; --space-8: 8px; --space-12: 12px; --space-16: 16px; --space-20: 20px; --space-24: 24px; --space-28: 28px; --space-32: 32px; --space-40: 40px; --space-48: 48px;
  --status-bar: 54px; --home-indicator: 34px; --top-bar: 44px; --tab-bar: 83px; --margin: 20px;
  --row: 52px; --row-detail: 59px;
  --radius-control: 999px; --radius-field: 999px; --radius-card: 20px; --hit: 44px;
  /* the kit draws no box shadows */
  --dur-fast: 120ms; --dur-base: 160ms; --dur-slow: 240ms; --ease: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1);
}
:root[data-theme="dark"] {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --canvas: #101116; --ink: #fafaf7; --muted: #9c9d9d;
  --line: #333438; --pri-tint: #1e202b; --error: #fafaf7;
  --on-error: #101116; --kit-primary-2: #b6b7b6; --kit-field: #8290c7;
  --kit-field-2: #ff7b72; --ab-muted: #9c9d9d; --success-icon: #dde1f2;
  --warning-icon: #f6cf6b; --kit-badge: #f8e2aa; --primary-text: #8290c7;
  --kit-stepper-text: #807f7e; --kit-feature: #8290c7; --accent-ink: #f6cf6b;
  --sec-tint: #313239; --info-icon: #8290c7;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { :root { --dur-fast: 0ms; --dur-base: 0ms; --dur-slow: 0ms; } }
```
Follow the system appearance:
```js
const darkMq = matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
const applyTheme = () => { document.documentElement.dataset.theme = darkMq.matches ? 'dark' : 'light'; };
applyTheme(); darkMq.addEventListener('change', applyTheme);
```

#### App icon (Locked)
A paper circle with a 1.5px ink line holding one solid primary dot. Deliver a 1024x1024 master without transparency or pre-rounded corners (the system masks it), a dark variant on `#101116` and a tinted variant (glyph only, one color), and derive every other size from the master. Web: also a 512px maskable icon and a 32px favicon from the same master.

## Part 2: Design tokens

These are the brand's tokens: use them as given, and use nothing else for colour, type or spacing. The first block is the same file "Save + files" writes; the last block holds the values of every custom property the Part 3 CSS reads, resolved on the kit root, so every `var(--x)` in Part 3 has a value here.

### tokens.css

```css
:root {

  --space-4: 4px; --space-8: 8px; --space-12: 12px; --space-16: 16px; --space-20: 20px; --space-24: 24px; --space-28: 28px; --space-32: 32px; --space-40: 40px; --space-48: 48px;
```
